class=\"meta\">先完成选择，再查看解析。练习内容不计入词汇正文覆盖。</p><p class=\"meta\" data-score=\"16\"></p><button type=\"button\" data-reset=\"16\">清空本章作答，重新自测</button><div class=\"question\" id=\"q-17-1\" data-question=\"16,0\"><p class=\"meta\">1. inference</p><p><strong>Why does Mara refuse to restart the demonstration immediately after the mechanism speeds up?</strong></p><div class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. She has decided that the object is permanently beyond repair.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. She hopes the panel will cancel every other demonstration.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. She wants the changed conditions understood before risking further damage.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. She believes no result obtained in public can be trusted.</button></div><div class=\"feedback\" hidden=\"hidden\"></div></div></section><nav class=\"chapter-nav\" aria-label=\"Chapter navigation\"><button data-go=\"15\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"17\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"v02c06","title":"Assistant Eleven","volume":"卷二｜谎言学院","anchor":"chapter-0018","paragraphs":["Cassian was identified because he knew how to fold a coat.","At least, that was the detail the woman from the donor committee chose to remember. Mara had left her jacket on a workshop chair. Cassian picked it up, checked the pocket containing her key, and folded it over his arm. The woman stopped in the doorway with a stack of programs and stared.","\"Your Grace?\"","The room became quiet in a way that was entirely different from concentration.","Cassian turned. \"Mrs. Pell.\"","\"I thought—\" She looked from his plain apron to Mara's numbered table. \"What are you doing here?\"","\"Carrying a coat. Before that, a mechanical theater.\"","Mara would have enjoyed the answer if she had not seen Lucien's expression. Surprise was giving way to something harder. Cassian's full name had been declared to the registrar, but being told that an applicant's assistant was a duke had a different bearing from discovering it behind the scenes.","Within an hour, someone had circulated a photograph. The caption called the fellowship an expensive disguise for a family arrangement. By the time Mara saw it, the story had acquired a guaranteed medal, a private donation, and a threat to overthrow the judging panel. There was no evidence for any of them. The absence of evidence did little to slow user traffic.","\"My congratulations,\" Ada said bitterly. \"You're now famous for a success you haven't had.\"","Mara stared at the image. Cassian's hand rested on the back of her chair. A moment of ordinary convenience had been customized into a portrait of control.","The dean called them to a meeting with the independent panel. Its chair, Dr. Estelle Hart, was an Australian conservator who had spent years arguing that restoration should be judged by methods rather than fashionable credentials. Her coat was pastel green. Her voice suggested she would be difficult to alienate accidentally and impossible to manipulate on purpose.","\"The registration is in accordance with the rules,\" Hart said. \"That does not settle the possible conflict. I want a clear account of assistance, advice, and contact with the judges.\"","Cassian supplied his record. He had carried the object, photographed Mara's work, entered observations she dictated, and disconnected the power on her instruction. He had not chosen a method, modified the mechanism, or contacted a judge outside the ordinary sessions. He described the unrelated missing-person inquiry separately.","\"Did you offer any interpretation of archival material?\" Hart asked.","\"I recognized an abbreviation in a date note. It resembled one in an accident file. I said that resemblance did not establish who had written it.\"","Mara saw the dean make a mark. She could have wished Cassian had omitted that answer. She respected him more for giving it.","Hart turned to her. \"Did that observation enter your condition report or demonstration?\"","\"No. It entered a request to preserve an old workbench. The request carries his professional signature. The fellowship material doesn't depend on it.\"","\"Then we'll verify the separation. I won't define every conversation between two people as technical assistance. Nor will I assume that the word fiancé makes assistance acceptable.\"","The panel allowed them to remain while it checked the records. A formal amendment required any future research advice to be logged with its purpose. Hart also asked Cassian to avoid the private reception that evening. The academy wanted to employ his public image in its publicity. A refusal would make the boundary easier to maintain.","\"I have already declined,\" he said.","Outside, Mara leaned against a cold wall. The relief was incomplete. She had passed the first inquiry only to learn that every kindness now required an explanatory note.","\"I could withdraw as your assistant,\" Cassian said.","She looked up sharply. \"Would that rectify what has already happened?\"","\"It might counterbalance the impression.\"","\"Or confirm that we think there's something to hide. Let me finish considering the options before you choose the sacrifice.\"","He went quiet. She recognized the effort. He was used to making a decision before a danger reached someone else. This danger, however, was hers to assess as well.","\"I'm trying to give you room,\" he said.","\"Then let me use it.\"","They stood apart for a moment. Mara had expected an argument and felt oddly disappointed when he gave her the silence she had requested. Independence could become a lonely habit if she mistook every offer of help for an attack on it.","She unfolded her arms. \"Would you mind staying until the panel decides? As Assistant Eleven. Not as a remedy for the rumors.\"","\"I can do that.\"","\"There may be more trays.\"","\"I have developed an informed concern about their size.\"","Her laughter came out suddenly, almost painfully. He smiled at the sound but did not touch her. They were learning the dynamics of a promise that had begun as paperwork and kept acquiring consequences neither had anticipated.","At lunch, Ada took them to the theater's old rehearsal hall. The building adjoined the academy's storage annex, but its entrance was on the road behind the kitchens. A damaged poster advertised a February production whose opening had been cancelled. The painter had left a ladder against the porch and gone unpaid.","Ada's mother sat inside, sorting costume buttons. She was recovering from abdominal surgery, and Ada had been bringing her to work rather than leave her alone all day. Mara introduced Cassian by name. Her mother did not react until he sat in a child's rehearsal chair and almost vanished behind his knees.","\"You need a larger part,\" she told him.","Ada covered her face. Cassian asked whether that was an artistic assessment or a safety warning. The old woman laughed so loudly that a stagehand came to see what had happened.","For a little while, they ate bread and watched the crew repair a door. The theater had once supported an abundance of evening classes, from computer programming to dental-model casting for local trainees. Its current curriculum had shrunk to whatever could keep the building open. The academy's online exhibition had drawn thousands of viewers, yet the physical collection was stored beneath a roof that leaked.","\"The writing is on the wall,\" Ada said, pointing to an actual water mark. \"They'll close us and call it an innovative digital service.\"","\"Who owns the annex?\" Mara asked.","\"A public trust. It can appoint a manager, not sell the building unilaterally. But a bank holds the repair debt. The management company says it can demand a refund of its investment.\"","Cassian asked to see the agreement. Then he stopped. \"Outside my assistant's duties, and only if you want an independent referral.\"","Ada glanced at Mara before answering. \"I want someone knowledgeable who doesn't tell me the building is a sentimental mistake.\"","\"I can find that person.\"","The distinction mattered. He was not promising a solution he could purchase at once. He was offering a way to obtain advice without surrendering control. Mara wished she had understood earlier how different those things could be.","When they returned to the workshop, a crowd was waiting. A mass-market news site had published the photograph. One commentator demanded that the academy take a tough line against hereditary privilege. Another accused it of persecuting a deserving donor. Neither appeared interested in the object on Mara's table.","Lucien stood by the door. \"You should have told us.\"","\"His name was registered.\"","\"I know. I mean you could have told the people working next to you. It would have spared us feeling foolish.\"","She wanted to call that unreasonable. After all, she had not hidden Cassian's face. But a legitimate objection could survive an imperfect expression. \"I didn't think about how you'd hear it,\" she said. \"I should have. That doesn't mean he did my work.\"","\"No,\" Lucien said. \"It doesn't.\"","He showed her a fresh copy of her report. The disputed paragraph had appeared in the portal again, although the dean had asked that it be separated from the original. This version was marked \"candidate confirmed.\" Mara had confirmed nothing.","Dr. Hart arrived before the crowd could turn the discovery into a spectacle. She ordered the relevant files preserved and arranged a formal hearing for the following morning. All affected submissions would be checked, not just Mara's. The panel would hear testimony before reaching an overall judgment.","\"Until then,\" she said, \"no contestant is to be publicly described as dishonest. Questions about the process are necessary. Harassment isn't.\"","Mara's throat tightened. She could resist a cruel accusation. It was harder to prepare herself for a fair hearing whose result remained genuinely uncertain.","Cassian offered to take the theater back to the workbench. She nodded. He lifted it with the same care he had shown before anyone knew his title.","Ada watched him settle the base onto its clean cloth. \"I think I preferred the coat story,\" she said.","\"So did I,\" Mara replied."],"questions":[{"type":"scope","stem":"Why does Dr. Hart continue examining the situation although Cassian registered under his real name?","options":["A hereditary title automatically data-question=\"17,0\"><p class=\"meta\">1. scope</p><p><strong>Why does Dr. Hart continue examining the situation although Cassian registered under his real name?</strong></p><div class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. A hereditary title automatically makes an assistant ineligible.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. Registration proves that Mara received undisclosed technical advice.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. The panel has established that the registration was forged.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. Compliance with registration rules does not by itself resolve a possible conflict of interest.</button></div><div class=\"feedback\" hidden=\"hidden\"></div></div></section><nav class=\"chapter-nav\" aria-label=\"Chapter navigation\"><button data-go=\"16\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"18\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"v02c07","title":"What a Record Can Prove","volume":"卷二｜谎言学院","anchor":"chapter-0019","paragraphs":["The hearing took place in a seminar room rather than the auditorium. Dr. Hart said this was a question of evidence, not a performance. Someone from the publicity office objected that donors expected an explanation. She replied that they would receive one after she had something defensible to explain.","Mara sat at the end of a long table. Through the window, she could see two students playing badminton with a shuttlecock whose feathers were falling out. The sight made her absurdly envious. Their problems could be solved by buying another shuttlecock. Hers had acquired a file number and a committee.","Beside her, Ada arranged their notes. Cassian sat among the witnesses, not behind Mara's chair. They had agreed on that in advance. If she needed a moment, she could ask for one without the room mistaking his proximity for an instruction.","The panel had invited Dr. Austin Lin, a visiting computational researcher from the University of California, Berkeley. He was not there to identify a culprit. His role was to explain what the available system records could support. The distinction was stated at the start and repeated when a faculty representative asked whether artificial intelligence could reconstruct the missing history.","\"It could generate a plausible history,\" Lin said. \"That would not turn a prediction into a record. I need to know which data exist before I can tell you what they mean.\"","Mara liked him immediately.","The first allegation concerned plagiarism. The final paragraph of her report matched text in an unpublished thesis. The source paper had been held in the academy's document store, where candidates could not normally access it. A recommendation engine had, however, indexed its abstract. The disputed sentence was longer than the abstract and contained a technical claim absent from Mara's original notes.","\"Could she have seen the whole paper?\" asked a member of the academic senate.","\"There are a range of possibilities,\" Lin said. \"The present permissions are not proof of the permissions that existed yesterday. We should re-evaluate the access history, not assume either access or its absence.\"","The representative nodded, visibly disappointed by the lack of a clear villain.","Holt described receiving Mara's printed condition sheet. It did not contain the disputed paragraph. Her copy had been placed with the day's assessment material before the report circulated. That established the existence of an earlier version. It did not establish that Mara could not have added something later. Lin asked the panel to keep those propositions distinct.","When Mara was invited to speak, she said only what she could account for. She had prepared the condition notes, uploaded the file, and received an automatic receipt. She had not checked the final rendering until Lucien brought her his copy. That omission was hers. The paragraph was not.","\"You ask us to accept your character,\" the senate member said.","\"I ask you to preserve the records and compare them. You don't have to like me to do that.\"","The room went quiet. Mara felt the urge to apologize for sounding combative and let it pass. A strict question did not require an obedient smile.","Lin showed two sequences of events. The system recorded an upload under Mara's account. Later, a service account modified the document and generated the label \"candidate confirmed.\" The same label had appeared in other reports without a separate confirmation step. The logs identified the service, not the person or process that had supplied its input.","\"Is that a software error?\" Sayer asked.","\"It may be. It may also be an authorized feature used incorrectly, or a deliberate alteration. I cannot rank those explanations from these records alone.\"","A panel member wanted a rapid conclusion. Lin explained that speed would not improve the efficacy of an incomplete examination. They needed the service's source data, its configuration at the relevant time, and a reliable account of who could modify each. A screenshot of the current settings would not answer a question about an ever-changing system.","Mara recognized the temptation in the room. Everyone wanted an explanation they could carry in their heads. One criminal, one malfunction, one careless applicant. The empty space where the answer belonged made people restless.","The second allegation concerned outside assistance. Cassian gave his testimony without mentioning their agreement or the social cost of being called her fiancé. He identified the photographs he had taken and the point at which he had disconnected the power. He acknowledged that recognizing the date abbreviation had helped the separate inquiry. He did not portray himself as an expert restorer.","\"You hold an official position,\" the senate member said. \"Could your presence have affected a judge?\"","\"Yes. That is why I declared my identity and did not contact them privately. You should ask the judges about their decisions. I cannot testify to their minds.\"","Mara lowered her eyes to her notes. She had never heard restraint sound so sharply like courage.","Then Ada stood. She explained how the second catalogue had reached the museum store. The theater had served as a recipient of loaned equipment. Its registry contained delivery slips whose dates could be compared with the academy's book. She had brought copies with the retired manager's permission, excluding names unrelated to the inquiry.","The panel's clerk frowned. \"Why exclude anything?\"","\"Because one page lists families who applied for subsidized tuition,\" Ada said. \"Their financial difficulties don't become public because you need to check a delivery.\"","A murmur passed around the table. Mara saw Ada grip the back of her chair. Her employer had warned her that the theater might lose a contribution if she embarrassed a patron. She had not mentioned that warning until the walk to the hearing. Now she was refusing to buy credibility with somebody else's private information.","Hart agreed to inspect the originals privately if required. \"Accessibility of evidence and unrestricted publication aren't the same thing. We can preserve one without demanding the other.\"","The senate member shifted to the damaged screen. Mara had acknowledged an unsafe result in the preliminary demonstration. Why should the panel allow her to continue when the object had been exposed to additional stress?","She laid her hands flat on the table. \"Because the purpose of the next test would be to distinguish the controller's behavior from my intervention. I stopped as soon as the conditions changed. I accept responsibility for what I did. I won't accept a conclusion about what I didn't do without an examination.\"","\"And if the test shows your method was at fault?\"","\"Then I change the method.\"","She heard the answer settle in the room. It was not a promise of victory. It was the only promise she could honestly make.","The panel withdrew. Ada went to telephone her mother. Cassian remained in his witness chair until Mara crossed the room and sat beside him.","\"I wanted to interrupt him,\" she said.","\"So did I.\"","\"You looked calm.\"","\"I have a great deal of practice looking unlike my intentions.\"","Her shoulders eased. \"Were any of those intentions particularly disgraceful?\"","\"The chair under him was poorly balanced. I considered allowing him to discover the fact.\"","She laughed quietly. The sound felt almost disloyal to the morning's fear. Then she realized that fear did not deserve exclusive use of the day.","When the panel returned, Hart read its decision. The current evidence was insufficient to attribute the added text to Mara or to identify another author. The allegation would remain unresolved while an independent examiner preserved and studied the relevant data. The label \"candidate confirmed\" was to be removed from public display because it overstated what the record established.","Mara could continue conditionally. Her next demonstration would take place under controlled, independently documented conditions. A separate examiner would observe every intervention. Cassian could remain an assistant, but he would not handle the control interface. The panel would assess method and result without using his status as evidence for either merit or misconduct.","It was not exoneration. It was an opportunity to produce something stronger than competing accounts.","Outside, Ada's phone rang again. She listened, went very still, and thanked the caller in a voice that had lost its expression.","\"My shift has been cancelled,\" she said after hanging up. \"They say the theater can no longer fund unnecessary registry work.\"","Mara reached for her arm, then stopped. \"What do you want to do?\"","Ada looked back at the academy. \"Get paid for the hours I've already worked. Keep the copies safe. And finish this properly.\"","Cassian asked whether she wanted the name of an independent employment adviser. She nodded. There was no grand promise to make the layoff vanish, and Mara was grateful for that. What had happened would not be repaired by pretending it had cost nothing.","On the steps, Lucien caught up with them. He carried his own tool case and a paper packet.","\"My spare control unit,\" he said. \"The examiner can inspect it before anyone touches it. I don't want to win because your machine was tested by different rules.\"","Mara took the packet. \"You might still beat me.\"","\"I intend to. Preferably without a committee explaining that I only won by default.\"","For the first time that morning, the next day seemed like something other than a threat."],"questions":[{"type":"inference","stem":"Why does Ada omit family financial details from the delivery-record copies she brings to the hearing?","options":["She wants to conceal a contradictory delivery date from the panel.","Those details are not necessary to check the delivery, and she wants to protect the families.","She believes private records can never be used as evidence.","She has no originals that could be examined."],"answer":1,"explanation":"她说明核验交付并不需要公开申请补贴家庭的经济困难；Hart随后允许必要时私下核对原件，体现相关性与隐私的区分。","evidence":[27],"shuffled":true}],"wordCount":1541,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0019\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">卷二｜谎言学院</div><h1>What a Record Can Prove</h1><p id=\"chapter-0019-p1\"><a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">The</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-3865\" data-words=\"3865\">hearing</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8263\" data-words=\"8263\">took place</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-4167\" data-words=\"4167\">in</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-1\" data-words=\"1\">a</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-7393\" data-words=\"7393\">seminar</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-7157\" data-words=\"7157\">room</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-6738\" data-words=\"6738\">rather than</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">the</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-651\" data-words=\"651\">auditorium</a>. class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. She wants to conceal a contradictory delivery date from the panel.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. Those details are not necessary to check the delivery, and she wants to protect the families.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. She believes private records can never be used as evidence.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. She has no originals that could be examined.</button></div><div class=\"feedback\" hidden=\"hidden\"></div></div></section><nav class=\"chapter-nav\" aria-label=\"Chapter navigation\"><button data-go=\"17\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"19\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"v02c08","title":"The Conditions of a Fair Test","volume":"卷二｜谎言学院","anchor":"chapter-0020","paragraphs":["Ivo arrived with a small case of tools and a larger complaint about the academy's parking arrangements. He had been asked to assess a controller. The guard had directed his van towards the ornamental lake, beside a sign offering pedal boats to visitors. He considered this an unnecessarily aggressive greeting.","\"Nobody is asking you to go on the water,\" Mara said.","\"The sign had expectations.\"","Noor took his coffee before he could set it beside the apparatus. \"The experiment has enough variables already.\"","Dr. Hart had approved technical advice, provided it was recorded and available on the same terms to the remaining contestants. Ivo would examine the controller, not repair Mara's artifact or select its conservation method. Another engineer, appointed by the panel, would supervise his work. Cassian carried the equipment to the laboratory, then stood back with the air of a man beginning to specialize in controlled noninterference.","Ada came in late. She had spent the morning collecting her final pay slip from the theater. The manager would not give her a written explanation of the cancelled shifts. She had requested one by email and saved her request. It was a small, necessary piece of accounting for a loss she could not yet measure.","Mara pulled out a chair. \"Do you want to leave this for today?\"","\"No. I want something to behave according to its own instructions. A machine will do.\"","Ivo opened the case. \"An ideal that has motivated generations of engineers. Usually in vain.\"","The panel's guideline was simple: the original theater would remain disconnected. They would test the shared controller with a harmless replacement load. A gauge would measure the output independently. Every input, adjustment, and result would be recorded from more than one angle. If a disagreement arose, they would stop before someone tried to explain it away.","Mara had expected a complicated arrangement. The physical test fitted on one table. Its reliability would depend on the quality of the record, not the glamour of the equipment. Ivo fixed a small motor to a stand, attached a visible marker, and showed the independent engineer how the speed could be measured without trusting the controller's own display.","\"We begin with the manual setting,\" he said. \"Then we load the same saved profile used yesterday. One change at a time.\"","A senior researcher asked why that sequence mattered.","\"Because if I change four things and the result changes, I don't know which one caused it. I can invent an anecdote. I can't give you an answer.\"","Mara glanced at Noor, who was trying not to look delighted.","The first test went exactly as expected. The display read twelve. The motor followed the selected rate. The second test used Lucien's spare unit and produced the same result. Nobody applauded. A correct intermediate step was allowed to be ordinary.","Then the examiner imported the saved profile. The display changed to twenty-four. The motor doubled its speed.","Ada let out a breath. Cassian did not move. Mara watched the marker until the examiner switched off the power, then looked down at her hands. They were clenched so tightly that her nails had left little lunar marks in her palms.","\"The effect is reproduced,\" Hart said. \"Record the conditions.\"","Ivo wanted another test. Mara wanted one too. Relief could infect judgment as easily as fear. They restored the starting settings, checked them independently, and repeated the import. The same change occurred. With a new profile created from the present settings, it did not.","\"An older instruction overrides the current input,\" Ivo said. \"We should look into the history of the saved profile. That isn't the same as looking for a particular person to blame.\"","The maintenance note had described a calibration problem. The new test showed how that problem could appear under specified conditions. It did not show who had supplied Mara's profile, whether the error was known to that person, or whether a different fault had also existed. Even so, the result changed the premise of the argument. Her incomplete demonstration could no longer be treated as straightforward proof of an unsafe restoration method.","Holt allowed herself a brief smile. \"You were right to stop.\"","Mara had imagined hearing those words would make the whole week disappear. Instead, she felt tired. The crack in the screen remained. Ada still had no shifts. An assertion, once made loudly, did not always vanish when its basis weakened.","Lucien inspected the written result. \"Will the judges see this?\"","\"Everyone will,\" Hart said. \"The full result and its limits. We won't replace one exaggerated claim with another.\"","From this point onwards, the final demonstrations would use independently checked controls. The revised rule would go into effect immediately and apply to every entrant. The academy would provide the same recording arrangement for all, including those who had not raised an objection. Mara liked that better than a private exception. A fair correction ought to outlive the person who demanded it.","While the engineers sealed the original controller, Bellamy arrived with a replacement screen. It was modern material, compatible with the theater's lamp, and cut to the correct dimensions. He set it beside Mara's table as though the gesture should terminate the discussion.","\"Use this,\" he said. \"You can still present a functioning object.\"","She held it against the old frame. The new material was clear and strong. It would also alter the shadow image, erase the evidence of the original construction, and hide what had happened to the old screen.","\"I'd like permission to compare the materials first.\"","Bellamy's expression tightened. \"You can be too cautious.\"","\"I know. I can also be too grateful. I don't want either feeling to make the choice for me.\"","Holt examined the edge of the original. The crack had begun in an older area of damage and extended under heat. A reversible support might prevent further movement. The final image would still show a faint line. Mara would have to explain why preserving that line was preferable to replacing the entire surface.","She decided to test a sample support on a separate piece of matching material. The conservation method remained hers to justify. Ivo left the table when she began. Cassian passed her the tray she requested and did not look towards Bellamy. Ada recorded the time.","The first sample failed. The second held but changed the way the light passed through it. Mara wrote both results down. The third, using less pressure and a narrower support, left the image imperfect but readable. She felt no sudden revelation, only the slow recognition that she had reached a method she could defend.","At sunset, the researcher brought a follow-up from the document examiner. The service that had altered her report had also imported external catalogue references from an old archive package. The examiner needed permission to inspect that package without revealing unrelated personal records. Hart had asked the academy to approve the request. Until then, nobody could state where the disputed paragraph had originated.","\"We're getting somewhere,\" Ada said.","\"At the speed of a very responsible snail,\" Mara replied.","\"A snail with an audit trail.\"","Cassian's mouth curved. Mara looked away before her own smile gave her entirely away.","After the others left, she found him in the courtyard, holding her coat. There was no donor committee now, no camera, and no need for an official record of who offered which hand. The city lights were appearing beyond the wall. A squirrel climbed the lantern sculpture, found nothing worth stealing, and abandoned the project.","\"A sensible colleague,\" Mara said.","Cassian followed her gaze. \"Its risk assessment is unusually strict.\"","She took the coat but did not put it on. \"When they questioned you, you could have made yourself seem less involved.\"","\"By leaving something out?\"","\"You'd have been very good at it.\"","\"Yes.\"","The honest answer warmed her more than a denial would have. He was not claiming that restraint came naturally or that he had never used his position. He had made a choice in that room, and she had seen it.","\"Thank you for choosing the difficult answer,\" she said.","He looked at her for a moment. \"Thank you for letting me remain after I offered to leave.\"","A thousand possible replies seemed to scatter in her head. She chose the smallest. \"There are still trays.\"","\"Then our arrangement continues.\"","Before she could decide what that sentence meant to her, Holt opened the workshop door. The independent test had been accepted. Mara would give her final restoration demonstration in the auditorium the following afternoon. The judging panel would see the full record of the failure, the test, and the repair.","Holt held out a sealed folder. \"One more thing. Bellamy says the workbench was sanded before an equipment auction. He has supplied the order.\"","\"Does it explain the removed number?\" Mara asked.","\"It explains why the surface was worked on. The rest still needs checking.\"","Inside the folder was a copy of the auction inventory. Bench Seventeen was listed for transport to an empty hospital wing in the theater district. Its destination code matched the surviving letters on the paper strip.","Mara looked back towards the room where the little theater waited under its dust cover. Tomorrow she had to make it work. After that, she needed to find out what else the academy had sent away."],"questions":[{"type":"scope","stem":"What does the independently recorded controller test establish?","options":["Mara's
